Title: Avertissements prophétiques d'Élie Marion, l'un des chefs des Protestans qui avoient pris les armes dans les Cévennes; ou Discours prononcez par sa bouche sous l'opération de l'Esprit
Creator: Élie Marion
Publisher: London (the imprint is not legible in the scan)
Publication Date: 1707
Rights: Public Domain (published 1707, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item bim_eighteenth-century_avertissements-prophetiq_marion-lie_1707 (https://archive.org/details/bim_eighteenth-century_avertissements-prophetiq_marion-lie_1707), the item's own bim_eighteenth-century_avertissements-prophetiq_marion-lie_1707_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Internet Archive; host metadata date: 1707. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: c24caef50f44289d38291fa604d84c5b2677ac3611475931f37a0f25b0d64607.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. British Library microfilm scan (Eighteenth Century Collections Online); the opening leaves and the title page are garbled, the warnings readable with the long s kept.
Content note: The utterances of the Camisard leader Élie Marion in London in 1706-1707, taken down by his scribes as he spoke in ecstasy, each dated; the prophetic voice of the movement carried into exile.
